Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Let $0<p<q\leq 1$. We show that the Schur $p$-property provides a powerful structural principle for Lipschitz-free $p$-spaces. Our main result asserts that $\mathcal{F}_p(M)$ has the Schur $p$-property for every $q$-metric space $M$; when $M$ is compact, it has the strong Schur $p$-property, with a constant depending only on $p$ and $q$. As consequences, $\mathcal{F}_p(M)$ is $\ell_p$-saturated, contains no isomorphic copy of an infinite-dimensional $r$-Banach space for $p<r\leq 1$, and every bounded operator from a $p$-Banach space into $\mathcal{F}_p(M)$ is either compact or fixes a copy of $\ell_p$. These results settle Questions 6.1, 6.2, 6.5, and 6.6 from our recent work [F. Albiac, J. L. Ansorena, J. Bíma and M. Cúth, Lipschitz free p-spaces for $0<p<1$ in the light of the Schur $p$-property and the compact reduction, J. Geom. Anal. 36 (2026), Paper No. 54] on the Schur $p$-property, together with several related problems. They also confirm a prediction of Kalton and the first-named author from 2009: no infinite-dimensional $q$-Banach space has the $p$-Lipschitz lifting property. Further applications reveal a sharp contrast between the linear and Lipschitz structures of nonlocally convex spaces.

</details>


### [96] [A Compact Counterexample to Suárez's Berezin Approximation Question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10846)
*Mishko Mitkovski,Yuyuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文否定地回答了 Suárez 2005 年关于高阶 Berezin 变换诱导映射在算子范数下收敛性的问题，并构造出紧凑算子使该映射范数无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Suárez 在 2005 年提问：对全 Bergman Toeplitz 代数中的每个算子 S，由 m 阶高阶 Berezin 变换诱导的映射 Q_m(S) 是否在算子范数下收敛到 S。本文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 利用一个移动矩阵边缘（moving matrix edge）构造障碍；采用一簇移动的秩一测试算子，每个算子支撑在第 m 个矩阵列上；在相应边缘附近，这些测试算子被映为加权 Hankel 矩阵，其极限系数构成显式的 Pascal 核；进而证明独立的 Pascal–Hankel 定理，说明相关加权 Hankel 变换不能有界地把 ℓ^2 映入迹类；最后借助有限截面收敛、迹对偶与一致有界性原理，将这种不稳定性传递到精确的 Bergman 映射。

Result: 存在一个紧凑算子 S，使得 sup_{m≥0} ||Q_m(S)|| = ∞；特别地，沿严格递增序列 (m_n)，有 ||Q_{m_n}(S) − S|| → ∞。因此原问题以强形式被否定回答。

Conclusion: Q_m(S) 在算子范数下一般并不收敛到 S；即使 S 为紧凑算子也可能出现范数发散，表明高阶 Berezin 变换诱导映射在算子范数意义下缺乏稳定性。

Abstract: Let $A^2(\mathbb D)$ be the unweighted Bergman space and write $Q_m(S)=T_{B_m(S)}$ for the map induced by the $m$th higher-order Berezin transform. Suárez asked in 2005 whether $Q_m(S)$ converges to $S$ in operator norm for every $S$ in the full Bergman Toeplitz algebra. We answer this question negatively in a strong form: there is a compact operator $S$ such that \[
  \sup_{m\geq 0}\|Q_m(S)\|=\infty. \] In particular, along a strictly increasing sequence $(m_n)$ one has \[
  \|Q_{m_n}(S)-S\|\longrightarrow\infty. \]
  The obstruction is a moving matrix edge. The proof uses a moving family of rank-one test operators, each supported in the $m$th matrix column. Near the corresponding edge, these test operators are sent to weighted Hankel matrices, and the limiting coefficients form an explicit Pascal kernel. We prove a standalone Pascal--Hankel theorem showing that the associated weighted Hankel transformation fails to map $\ell^2$ boundedly into trace class. Finite-section convergence, trace duality, and the Uniform Boundedness Principle then transfer this instability to the exact Bergman maps.

</details>


### [97] [Antitonicity property of the Moore-Penrose inverse for selfadjoint operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10757)
*Guillermina Fongi,M. Celeste Gonzalez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究自伴算子的Moore-Penrose逆关于Löwner序的反单调性，利用正分解，并将该性质的一个弱化版本与Thompson分量联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moore-Penrose逆的反单调性在矩阵情形已有一定研究，但在自伴算子类中、相对于Löwner序的系统刻画尚不完整，需要借助算子自身的正分解工具来深入分析。同时，反单调性的弱化形式与Thompson分量之间的关系此前未被充分揭示，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在自伴算子类中，相对于Löwner序考察Moore-Penrose逆的反单调性。核心手段是利用自伴算子所允许的各种正分解（positive decompositions）来处理问题；此外，引入反单调性的一个弱化版本，并将其与Thompson分量（Thompson components）建立联系。

Result: 通过不同的正分解刻画了自伴算子Moore-Penrose逆的（反）单调性行为；并证明了反单调性弱化版本与Thompson分量之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 正分解是研究自伴算子Moore-Penrose逆反单调性的有效工具，反单调性的弱化形式可由Thompson分量刻画，从而推广和补充了 Moore-Penrose 逆单调性理论。

Abstract: In this article we study the antitonicity property of the Moore-Penrose inverse in the class of selfadjoint operators, with respect to the Löwner order. For this purpose, we employ different positive decompositions that selfadjoint operators admit. In addition, we relate a weak version of the antitonicity property with Thompson components.

</details>


### [98] [A General Proof of the Fong-Tsui Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10797)
*Yicen Ma*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在一般复Hilbert空间上有界算子框架下证明了Fong-Tsui猜想：若|T|≤|Re T|，则T为自伴算子，并给出定量的稳定性估计。证明结合Sylvester映射的正逆与谱截断技术，无需紧性、迹类或可分性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fong-Tsui猜想是算子理论中的一个公开问题，此前结果多依赖紧性、迹类或可分性等附加假设。作者希望给出一个在任意复Hilbert空间上、对一般有界算子成立的普适证明，并进一步获得带误差项的定量稳定性刻画。

Method: 将Sylvester映射的正逆与由正缺陷|Re T|-|T|的范数确定的谱截断相结合；通过局部消失引理把分析化归为经典的平方自伴性判据；利用缺陷的正性导出全局范数矛盾；将论证抽象为四算子消失原理；并推导定量稳定性估计。

Result: 证明了|T|≤|Re T|蕴含T自伴；建立了四算子消失原理，且不依赖紧性、迹或可分性假设；得到定量稳定性：当|T|≤|Re T|+εI且0≤ε≤|T|时，|Im T|≤6|T|^{7/8}ε^{1/8}，其中常数与维数无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为Fong-Tsui猜想提供了一个结构清晰、假设极弱的统一证明，核心是Sylvester映射正逆与谱截断的结合及四算子消失原理。定量稳定性估计给出了缺陷范数对虚部范数的显式控制，常数与维数无关，但指数未声称最优。文中还声明使用了大型语言模型辅助证明开发、代数计算、数值检验与论证审计。

Abstract: We present a general proof of the Fong-Tsui conjecture for bounded operators on arbitrary complex Hilbert spaces. Specifically, we show that $|T|\leq|\operatorname{Re}T|$ implies that $T$ is self-adjoint. The argument combines a positive inverse of a Sylvester map with a spectral cutoff determined by the norm of the positive defect $|\operatorname{Re}T|-|T|$. A local vanishing lemma reduces the analysis to the classical squared self-adjointness criterion, while positivity of the defect yields a global norm contradiction. We formulate the argument as an abstract four-operator vanishing principle, without compactness, trace, or separability assumptions. We also establish a quantitative stability estimate: if $|T|\leq|\operatorname{Re}T|+\varepsilon I$ and $0\leq\varepsilon\leq|T|$, then $|\operatorname{Im}T|\leq6|T|^{7/8}\varepsilon^{1/8}$. The constant is independent of the dimension, and the exponent is not claimed to be optimal. Large language models (LLMs) were used to assist with proof development, algebraic calculations, numerical checks, and auditing of the arguments.

</details>


### [99] [Zonoids whose polars are zonoids: the Banach--Mazur distance need not tend to one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10852)
*Dmitry Ryabogin,Artem Zvavitch*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究高斯旋转带体Zn，证明其对偶Zn*仍是带体，计算其到欧氏球的Banach-Mazur距离约为1.10且与维数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究所有极体仍为带体的带体类中Banach-Mazur距离的上确界是否趋于1，并寻求X与X*均可等距嵌入L1的非Hilbert可分Banach空间实例。

Method: 构造由Vitale与Mathis工作导出的高斯旋转带体Zn，证明Zn*亦为带体，并计算Zn到欧氏球的Banach-Mazur距离。

Result: 该距离与维数无关，约为1.10；极体为带体的带体类中Banach-Mazur距离的上确界不收敛于1；构造出可分实Banach空间X，非等距于Hilbert空间，且X与X*均线性等距嵌入L1。

Conclusion: 带体与其极体的几何关系可产生非平凡的Banach-Mazur距离下界，并给出X与X*同时等距嵌入L1的非Hilbert空间例子。

Abstract: For every $n\geq2$, we consider a Gaussian zonoid of revolution $Z_n$ arising from works of Vitale and Mathis. We prove that $Z_n^*$ is also a zonoid and compute the Banach--Mazur distance from $Z_n$ to the Euclidean ball. This distance is independent of the dimension and is approximately $1.10$. Consequently, the supremal Banach--Mazur distance among zonoids whose polars are zonoids does not converge to $1$. The construction also produces a separable real Banach space $X$, not isometric to a Hilbert space, such that both $X$ and $X^*$ embed linearly isometrically into $L_1$.

</details>


### [100] [Typical dynamical properties of operators on $\ell_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10957)
*Jian Li,Qijing Liao*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究 $\ell_p$ 空间上有界线性算子族 $\mathcal{L}_M(X)$ 在 SOT$^*$ 拓扑下的典型动力学性质，并刻画具体族 $\mathcal{M}=\{I+B_w\}$ 的典型性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超循环算子在受范数约束的算子集合中的"典型"（Baire 范畴意义下剩余）动力学行为，厘清弱混合、拓扑遍历、多重不交超循环等性质在典型算子上的表现，并针对具体的加权移位族给出相应刻画。

Method: 在 $X=\ell_p$（$1<p<\infty$）上，对 $\mathcal{L}_M(X)$ 赋予 SOT$^*$ 拓扑，利用 Baire 范畴与剩余集方法证明典型算子满足若干动力学性质；对 $\mathcal{M}=\{I+B_w\}$（$B_w$ 为双侧加权后移）赋予范数拓扑，同样分析其典型动力学性质。

Result: 在 SOT$^*$ 下，典型算子 $T\in\mathcal{L}_M(X)$ 是弱混合的、与给定超循环算子 $S$ 弱不交、非拓扑遍历，且对任意 $k\geq 2$，$(T,T^2,\dots,T^k)$ 是不交超循环的；同时给出族 $\mathcal{M}$ 在范数拓扑下的典型动力学性质。

Conclusion: 典型算子表现出强混合性（弱混合、多重不交超循环）与遍历性缺失（非拓扑遍历）并存的特征，说明约束范数下的算子集在 SOT$^*$ 拓扑中具有丰富的动力学结构。

Abstract: We investigate the typical dynamical properties of hypercyclic operators in $\mathcal{L}_M(X)$, the set of all bounded linear operators on $X$ whose norms are at most $M$, when $X=\ell_p$, $1< p<\infty$. We show that, with respect to SOT$^*$, a typical operator $T\in \mathcal{L}_M(X)$ is weakly mixing, is weakly disjoint from a given hypercyclic operator $S$, is not topologically ergodic, and satisfies $(T,T^2,\dotsc,T^k)$ is disjoint hypercyclic for any $k\geq 2$. We also study the typical dynamical properties for the concrete family $\mathcal{M}=\{I+B_w\in \mathcal{L}(X)\colon w\in c_0(\mathbb{Z})\}$, endowed with the norm topology, where $B_w$ is a bilateral weighted backward shift.

</details>


### [101] [A bi-Lipschitz characterization of strong minimum-attainment for Lipschitz maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11080)
*Geunsu Choi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了强最小可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性，证明其在完备度量空间上不稠密当且仅当该空间可双Lipschitz嵌入到具有正Lebesgue测度的 $\mathbb{R}$ 子集中（等价于具有正一维Hausdorff测度），并据此给出纯1-不可矫正性的等距刻画及向量值情形下的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强范数可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性已有研究，其"最小"类比——强最小可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性尚未被刻画。作者希望用双Lipschitz嵌入的几何语言来完整描述这一逼近性质何时失效，并将结果与度量空间的不可矫正性（unrectifiability）联系起来。

Method: 以双Lipschitz嵌入为工具，将强最小可达Lipschitz函数集的稠密性转化为度量空间 $M$ 的几何条件。主定理通过证明"不稠密"等价于" $M$ 双Lipschitz等价于 $\mathbb{R}$ 中正Lebesgue测度子集"，并利用1维Hausdorff测度给出等价表述；进而用双Lipschitz副本上闭子集的强最小可达映射刻画纯1-不可矫正性，并构造向量值反例。

Result: 主结果：完备度量空间 $M$ 上强最小可达Lipschitz函数集不稠密，当且仅当 $M$ 双Lipschitz等价于 $\mathbb{R}$ 的具有正Lebesgue测度的子集，等价于 $M$ 可双Lipschitz嵌入 $\mathbb{R}$ 且具有正1维Hausdorff测度。由此得到纯1-不可矫正性的等距刻画。同时给出若干反例说明该主结果不能自然推广到向量值情形。

Conclusion: 强最小可达Lipschitz函数的稠密性可由双Lipschitz嵌入与1维Hausdorff测度完全刻画，并可为纯1-不可矫正性提供等距特征；但该刻画本质上是标量值现象，向量值情形下不成立。

Abstract: We completely characterize the denseness of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz functions, a minimum analogue for strongly norm-attaining Lipschitz functions, in terms of bi-Lipschitz embeddings. More precisely, our main result shows that the set of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz functions defined on a complete metric space $M$ fails the denseness if and only if $M$ is bi-Lipschitz equivalent to a subset of $\mathbb{R}$ with positive Lebesgue measure, or equivalently, if $M$ admits a bi-Lipschitz embedding into $\mathbb{R}$ and $M$ has positive 1-dimensional Hausdorff measure. As a consequence, we provide an isometric characterization of the pure 1-unrectifiability of $M$ in terms of strongly minimum-attaining Lipschitz maps defined on bi-Lipschitz copies of closed subsets of $M$. Several counterexamples showing that the main result cannot be naturally extended to the vector-valued setting are also presented.

</details>


### [102] [On weak dominance of t-conorms over t-norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11150)
*Shuangquan Li,Yao Ouyang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了连续t-余模弱支配连续t-模的完整刻画，通过化归到序和算子的单个Archimedean分量，并用加性生成元表达弱支配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续t-余模对连续t-模的弱支配一般性判据尚未完全明确，已有结果主要局限于Archimedean与连续情形。

Method: 先将序和算子的弱支配问题化归为其单个Archimedean分量的问题，再借助这些分量的加性生成元完整刻画弱支配条件。

Result: 得到了连续t-余模弱支配连续t-模的完整刻画，方法统一覆盖严格型与幂零型情形，并可作为特例恢复Archimedean算子的已知结论。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了连续t-余模弱支配连续t-模的判据，提供了统一且更一般的刻画框架。

Abstract: The weak dominance of aggregation operators, particularly between triangular norms (t-norms) and triangular conorms (t-conorms), has attracted considerable attention in aggregation operator theory. While several characterizations have been obtained for Archimedean and continuous cases, a general criterion for continuous t-conorms over continuous t-norms remains to be fully clarified. In this paper, we provide a complete characterization of a continuous t-conorm weakly dominating a continuous t-norm. We first reduce the problem for ordinal sum operators to that for their single Archimedean components, and then express the weak dominance condition entirely in terms of the additive generators of these components. Our approach covers both strict and nilpotent cases uniformly, and recovers the known results for Archimedean operators as a special case.

</details>


### [103] [Saturation and Localization Results for Max-product Generalized Sampling Operators based on Centered Bell-shaped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11157)
*Lorenzo Boccali,Gianluca Vinti*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究max-product广义采样算子对非负有界一致连续函数的均匀逼近，确定了饱和阶为1/w，并给出局部逆定理及强局部化结果，将既往仅针对sinc型核的结论推广至更广核类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coroianu和Gal此前仅在特定sinc型核下得到相关饱和与逆逼近结论，本文旨在将其推广到更一般的偶对称钟形核函数，从而揭示该算子类的本质逼近能力上限与光滑性刻画。

Method: 在核函数为偶中心钟形函数的假设下，采用max-product广义采样算子逼近R上的非负有界一致连续函数；通过构造性分析与局部化论证，证明饱和阶、刻画饱和类，并建立局部逆估计及截断算子的强局部化定理。

Result: 1/w为均匀饱和阶，饱和类恰为非负常函数，即非平凡函数无法突破1/w的逼近速率；若f在紧区间[a,b]上以1/w速率被逼近，则f在[a,c]（0<a<b，c∈[a,b)）或[c,b]（a<b<0，c∈(a,b]）上Lipschitz连续；对[0,1]上严格正有界函数，截断max-product广义采样算子序列具有强局部化性质。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了max-product广义采样算子的一般核逼近理论，给出饱和阶、饱和类、局部逆定理与强局部化结果，把Coroianu-Gal的sinc型核结论实质性地推广到更广泛的钟形核函数。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the saturation order and a local inverse result for the uniform approximation of non-negative, bounded, and uniformly continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$ by max-product generalized sampling operators based on suitable kernel functions. In particular, assuming that the kernel is an even centered bell-shaped function, we first show that $1/w$, $w>0$, is the uniform saturation order, with the corresponding saturation class coinciding with the class of non-negative constant functions. This means that $1/w$ is the best possible rate of convergence that the max-product generalized sampling operators can achieve when approximating non-trivial (i.e., non-constant) non-negative, bounded, and uniformly continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$. Moreover, it is known that, for Lipschitz continuous functions on $\mathbb{R}$, the approximation order is $1/w$ as $w \to +\infty$. Here, we show that this result can be locally reversed. Specifically, we prove that if $f$ can be approximated at the rate $1/w$ on a compact interval $[a,b]\subset\mathbb{R}$, then $f$ is Lipschitz continuous on $[a,c]$ for every $c \in [a,b)$ whenever $0<a<b$, and on $[c,b]$ for every $c \in (a,b]$ whenever $ a<b<0$. Finally, under the same assumptions on the kernel, we establish a strong localization result for sequences of truncated max-product generalized sampling operators in the case of strictly positive and bounded functions defined on $[0,1]$. All these results extend previous results of Coroianu and Gal, which were established only for specific sinc-type kernels, to a broader class of kernel functions.

</details>


### [104] [A note on the stability of Arens products under small perturbations of multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11379)
*Deepika Rajoriya*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该短讯研究Banach代数乘法结构的小扰动对二次对偶上两个Arens乘积的影响，证明Arens不规则性在小扰动下稳定，并证明与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数其Arens正则性在小扰动下稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Banach代数乘法结构的微小变化如何影响其二次对偶上的Arens乘积（即Arens正则性与Arens不规则性），从而理解这些性质在结构扰动下的稳健性。

Method: 通过考察Banach代数$\mathcal A$乘法结构的小扰动，分析其对二次对偶$\mathcal A^{**}$上两个Arens乘积的影响，采用扰动分析的方法进行研究。

Result: 证明了Arens不规则性在Banach代数乘法结构足够小的扰动下保持稳定；并进一步证明，对于与一致代数等价的Banach函数代数，Arens正则性在原Banach函数代数乘法结构的小扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: Arens不规则性与（在特定Banach函数代数情形下的）Arens正则性均是乘法结构小扰动下的稳定性质。

Abstract: In this short note, we study the small perturbations of multiplication structure of a Banach algebra $\mathcal A$, and its impact on the two Arens products on the bidual $\mathcal A^{**}$. We show that the property of being \say{Arens irregular} is stable under sufficiently small perturbation in the multiplication structure of a Banach algebra. Further we show that for any Banach function algebra which is equivalent to a uniform algebra, the Arens regularity is stable under small perturbation on the multiplication structure of the original Banach function algebra.

</details>


### [105] [Noncommutative commutant lifting, interpolation, and shift-invariant subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11410)
*Sibaprasad Barik,Samir Panja*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间框架下建立 Sarason 型交换提升定理，并用于求解非交换行球上的 Nevanlinna-Pick 插值问题；同时给出移位不变子空间的 Beurling-Lax-Halmos 型刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Sarason 交换提升定理与 Nevanlinna-Pick 插值理论在单位圆盘上已十分成熟，而其在非交换多变量（行球）设定下的推广尚不完善。作者希望在非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间这一自然函数空间框架中建立相应理论，从而统一并推广经典结果。

Method: 采用算子理论与函数空间方法：在非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间上证明 Sarason 型交换提升定理；借助交换提升与扩张（dilation）技巧，将非交换 Nevanlinna-Pick 插值问题转化为算子存在性问题加以求解；并利用扩张理论刻画移位不变子空间。

Result: 成功证明非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间上的 Sarason 型交换提升定理；解决了非交换行球上的 Nevanlinna-Pick 插值问题，并作为特例恢复开单位圆盘上的经典 Nevanlinna-Pick 插值定理；得到非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间移位不变子空间的 Beurling-Lax-Halmos 型刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非交换 Drury-Arveson 空间中的交换提升与插值理论，统一并推广了经典单变量结果，同时为移位不变子空间提供基于扩张技巧的结构刻画，具有进一步推广至更一般非交换算子空间与插值问题的潜力。

Abstract: We establish a Sarason-type commutant lifting theorem in the framework of noncommutative Drury-Arveson space and apply it to solve the noncommutative Nevanlinna-Pick interpolation problem on the noncommutative row ball. In particular, it recovers the classical Nevanlinna-Pick interpolation theorem over the open unit disc. We also provide a Beurling-Lax-Halmos-type characterization of shift-invariant subspaces of noncommutative Drury-Arveson space using dilation theoretic technique.

</details>


### [106] [Sharp Volume-Based Bounds for BMO Functions on Compact Domains and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11467)
*Marin Mišur*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文为紧支集BMO函数建立了精确的局部$L^r$界：将经典John-Nirenberg估计中依赖支集直径的因子替换为支集的Lebesgue测度，并在无消失振荡假设下给出证明与若干物理、算子论应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典John-Nirenberg不等式给出的$L^r$估计依赖支集直径，对细长、高离心率的区域而言这一量过于粗糙；真正合适的几何量应是支集的Lebesgue测度，因此需要建立以测度替代直径的精确局部估计。

Method: 利用非增重排（non-increasing rearrangement）方法，并借助Bennett-DeVore-Sharpley定理完成证明；整个过程不假设任何消失振荡（vanishing-oscillation）条件。

Result: 证明了紧支集BMO函数的局部$L^r$界中直径依赖可被Lebesgue测度依赖所替代，估计为sharp；并给出若干应用：管状邻域中的几何塌缩、Schrödinger算子的局部Cwikel-Lieb-Rozenblum型界，以及Calderón-Zygmund奇异积分局部作用的解耦估计。

Conclusion: 该工作以支集测度取代直径，得到锐利的局部$L^r$界，突破了经典估计在细长区域上的局限，并在几何、算子谱理论与调和分析中具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: We establish sharp localized $L^r$ bounds for compactly supported functions of bounded mean oscillation (BMO). Classical estimates obtained via the John-Nirenberg inequality bound the $L^r$ norm of such a function in terms of the diameter of its support. We show that the diameter dependence can be replaced by the Lebesgue measure of the support, the relevant quantity for thin, highly eccentric domains. The proof relies on non-increasing rearrangements and the Bennett-DeVore-Sharpley theorem, and requires no vanishing-oscillation hypothesis. We present physical and operator-theoretic applications: geometric collapse in tubular neighborhoods, a localized Cwikel-Lieb-Rozenblum bound for Schrödinger operators, and decoupling bounds for the localized action of Calderón-Zygmund singular integrals.

</details>


### [107] [Jacobians of BV homeomorphisms and their weak-* limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11502)
*Jakub Petr*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中，任何 $BV$ 同胚以及 $BV$ 同胚的弱-* 极限都不能改变 Jacobian 的符号：几乎处处 $J_f \geq 0$ 或几乎处处 $J_f \leq 0$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $BV$（有界变差）映射的 Jacobian 符号行为，特别是同胚及其弱-* 极限这类正则性较低的对象，以澄清在弱正则假设下映射的定向保持/翻转性质。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中考察 $BV$ 同胚，并分析 $BV$ 同胚序列的弱-* 极限，利用有界变差函数与 Jacobian 的相关分析工具证明其符号几乎处处不变。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中任意 $BV$ 同胚以及 $BV$ 同胚的弱-* 极限的 Jacobian 几乎处处非负或几乎处处非正，即符号不能改变。

Conclusion: $BV$ 同胚及其弱-* 极限在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中保持 Jacobian 符号的几乎处处一致性，表明即使在弱正则框架下定向行为仍受严格约束。

Abstract: We prove that in $\mathbb{R}^3$ any $BV$ homeomorphism as well as the weak-* limit of $BV$ homeomorphisms cannot change the sign of the Jacobian, i.e., we have $J_f \geq 0$ almost everywhere or $J_f \leq 0$ almost everywhere.

</details>


### [108] [The Local Embedding Problem for Hardy Spaces of Dirichlet Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11560)
*Bonan Chen,Xiang Fang,Feng Guo,Shengzhao Hou,Yizhou Shao,Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Dirichlet级数Hardy空间的局部嵌入问题，证明了该嵌入性质成立的精确阈值是 $p\ge 2$，而非此前猜测的偶数整数指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部嵌入问题是一个无量纲的迹问题：全局 $\mathscr{H}^p$ 范数是否控制临界线 $\operatorname{Re}s=1/2$ 上的局部 $L^p$ 质量。此前仅在 $p=2$ 及偶数指数 $p=2k$ 上已知成立，并被猜测有限正指数情形仅止于此。本文旨在给出完整的有限指数分类。

Method: 证明转入对偶指数 $q=p/(p-1)\in(1,2)$，通过精确频率分解分离出单个共振Euler乘积项；用保持协方差的替换将共享素因子上的矩估计约化为对数相关高斯场，并借助临界分枝随机游走界获得所需的多尺度衰减；再用有限循环平方函数估计组装共振尺度，最后通过Hardy商对偶将向量值界转化为临界线迹估计。

Result: 对每个 $2<p<\infty$，存在与素变量数目及选择无关的常数 $C_p<\infty$，使得所有Dirichlet多项式 $P$ 满足 $\sup_{\theta}\int_\theta^{\theta+1}|P(1/2+it)|^p\,dt\le C_p\|P\|_{\mathscr{H}^p}^p$。结合 $0<p<2$ 的已知反例，得到尖锐阈值：局部嵌入性质恰好对 $p\ge 2$ 成立。

Conclusion: 真正的阈值是 $p=2$ 而非偶数整数性。该结果通过已知等价关系，为若干经典问题给出相同的尖锐阈值，包括共形不变半平面嵌入、反向局部Carleson测度转移，以及特征零Gordon–Hedenmalm复合算子的有界性。

Abstract: We solve the local embedding problem for Hardy spaces of Dirichlet series, which is a dimension-free trace problem asking whether the global $\mathscr{H}^p$-norm controls local $L^p$-mass on the critical line $\operatorname{Re}s=1/2$. More precisely, for every $2<p<\infty$, there exists a constant $C_p<\infty$ such that every Dirichlet polynomial $P$ satisfies $$ \sup_{θ\in\mathbb{R}}\int_θ^{θ+1}\left|P\left(\frac12+it\right)\right|^p\,\mathrm{d}t\le C_p\left\lVert P\right\rVert_{\mathscr{H}^p}^{p}, $$ with $C_p$ independent of the number and choice of prime variables on which $P$ depends. Before the present work, the embedding was known at $p=2$ and, by taking integer powers, at the even exponents $p=2k$; it had been conjectured that these exhaust the finite positive cases above $2$. Together with the known failure for $0<p<2$, our theorem gives the sharp finite-exponent classification: the local embedding property holds exactly for $p\ge2$. Thus the true threshold is $p=2$, rather than even integrality.
  The proof passes to the dual exponent $q=p/(p-1)\in(1,2)$, where an exact frequency decomposition isolates a single resonant Euler-product term. A covariance-preserving replacement of the shared prime factors reduces the resulting moment estimate to a log-correlated Gaussian field, and a critical branching-random-walk bound supplies the required multiscale decay. A finite-cyclic square-function estimate assembles the resonant scales, and Hardy-quotient duality converts the resulting vector-valued bound into the critical-line trace. For $1\le p<\infty$, known equivalences give the same sharp threshold in several classical problems, including the conformally invariant half-plane embedding, the reverse local Carleson-measure transfer, and boundedness of all characteristic-zero Gordon--Hedenmalm composition operators.

</details>


### [109] [Completeness of constructible norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11782)
*Juan Rafael Acosta-Portilla,Armando Sánchez-Nungaray,Gerardo Ramos-Vázquez,Lizbeth Yolanda Garrido-Ramírez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文研究给定Banach空间上等价范数族模去共线关系后的商空间,并证明在Banach-Mazur型度量下该族构成完备度量空间;同时证明了Banach空间中凸集间有界Lipschitz映射构成的Banach空间上可构造范数族的完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Banach空间理论中,等价范数族及其度量结构是重要研究对象。作者希望借助Banach-Mazur型度量,考察等价范数族在模去共线关系后的商空间是否具有完备性,并将结论推广到有界Lipschitz映射空间上的可构造范数族。

Method: 对给定Banach空间上的等价范数族引入Banach-Mazur型度量,并以共线关系作商,验证该商空间在该度量下的完备性;随后针对Banach空间凸集间有界Lipschitz映射所构成的Banach空间,类似地证明其可构造范数族的完备性。

Result: 证明了等价范数族模去共线关系后在Banach-Mazur型度量下是完备度量空间;并证明了Banach空间中凸集间有界Lipschitz映射构成的Banach空间上的可构造范数族也是完备的。

Conclusion: 该工作表明等价范数族(及其可构造范数子族)在适当的Banach-Mazur型度量下具有良好的完备性结构,为范数族的度量几何研究提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In the present work we study for a given Banach space the quotient of the family of equivalent norms modulus the collinearity relationship and prove that under a Banach-Mazur type metric this family is a complete metric space, and we also prove the completeness of the family of constructible norms for the Banach space of bounded Lipschitzian mappings between convex sets of a Banach space.

</details>


### [110] [The critical Schatten exponent for the Berger-Coburn endpoint problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11853)
*Jani A. Virtanen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文确定了Toeplitz算子$T_g$的Schatten类成员资格控制热变换$g^{(1/4)}$有界性的精确指标为$p=1$，给出了最优常数$2^n$的迹公式估计，并证明对$p>1$不存在相应的$S_p$估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berger与Coburn证明Fock空间上$T_g$的有界性可控制$1/4<t<1$时的热变换$g^{(t)}$，并猜想端点$g^{(1/4)}$刻画有界性。Looi最近通过构造有界$T_g$但$g^{(1/4)}$无界的反例否定了该猜想。本文旨在寻找刻画$g^{(1/4)}$有界性的正确算子类条件，并确定相关估计中的最优常数。

Method: 利用迹类算子的强收敛性质：Berger-Coburn迹公式中的迹类算子在$t\downarrow1/4$时迹范数发散但强收敛于$2^nJ$；建立恒等式$g^{(1/4)}(a)=2^n\operatorname{tr}(T_gW_aJW_a^*)$并由此导出范数估计；用Baire纲论证构造反例；并计算Berger-Coburn估计的最优常数。

Result: 证明$p=1$是使$T_g\in S_p$强制$g^{(1/4)}$有界的精确Schatten指标；得到$\|g^{(1/4)}\|_\infty\leq2^n\|T_g\|_{S_1}$，且$2^n$最优，该界对$0<p\leq1$的$T_g\in S_p$同样成立；对$p>1$不存在相应的$S_p$估计，即使对紧支撑光滑符号亦不成立；Baire纲论证表明存在$T_g\in S_p$（对所有$p>1$）而$g^{(1/4)}$无界的容许符号，但未给出显式构造；此外确定了$1/4<t\leq1$时Berger-Coburn估计的最优常数。

Conclusion: $g^{(1/4)}$的有界性由$T_g$的迹类（$S_1$）性质精确刻画，而非Looi猜想中的有界性；$2^n$为最优常数且对$0<p\leq1$成立；$p>1$时不存在对应的Schatten估计，并通过Baire纲方法证明了反例的存在性。

Abstract: Berger and Coburn showed that boundedness of a Toeplitz operator $T_g$ on the Fock space controls the heat transform $g^{(t)}$ for $1/4<t<1$, and conjectured the endpoint $g^{(1/4)}$ characterizes boundedness. Looi recently disproved this by constructing a bounded $T_g$ with unbounded $g^{(1/4)}$. We show that $p=1$ is the exact Schatten exponent for which $T_g\in S_p$ forces $g^{(1/4)}$ to be bounded. The trace-class operators appearing in Berger and Coburn's trace formula have divergent trace norms as $t\downarrow1/4$, yet converge strongly to $2^n J$, and it follows that $$
  g^{(1/4)}(a)=2^n\operatorname{tr}
  \bigl(T_gW_aJW_a^*\bigr),\qquad
  \|g^{(1/4)}\|_\infty\leq2^n\norm{T_g}_{S_1}, $$ for every admissible symbol $g$ with $T_g$ trace class, where $J$ is the parity operator and $W_a$ is Weyl translation. We prove that $2^n$ is optimal, and the same bound holds for $T_g\in S_p$ with $0<p\leq1$. For $p>1$, no corresponding $S_p$ estimate is possible, even for compactly supported smooth symbols. Moreover, a Baire category argument shows there is an admissible symbol $g$ with $T_g\in S_p$ for every $p>1$ while $g^{(1/4)}$ is unbounded, though it yields no explicit symbol. We also determine the optimal constants in the Berger--Coburn estimates for $1/4<t\leq1$.

</details>


### [111] [Improved Discrete Dual $p$-Hardy and Weighted Discrete $p$- Birman Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11890)
*Bikram Das*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立带位移的一维广义离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式，据此改进两个离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式、给出带幂权重的离散 $p$-Birman 不等式，并建立具有最佳常数的多变量对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式及其加权推广已有最佳常数，但缺乏更精细的改进形式；同时多变量情形的最佳常数对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式尚待建立。作者希望借助带位移的广义离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式统一处理这些问题。

Method: 首先证明带位移的一维广义离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式；以此为工具，对 $p>1$、$A\in C_c(\mathbb{N}_0)$ 且 $A_0=A_1=0$ 的情形改进基本不等式及其幂型加权推广（$p-1<\alpha\le p$），并保持常数最佳；随后建立带幂权重的离散 $p$-Birman 不等式；对于多变量情形，先证明两变量不等式，再推广到多变量并保持常数最佳性。

Result: 得到改进后的离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式及其幂型加权形式，相关常数项均为最佳；建立了带幂权重的离散 $p$-Birman 不等式；并给出具有最佳常数的多变量对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式。

Conclusion: 带位移的广义离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 不等式可作为统一工具，用于改进经典离散对偶 $p$-Hardy 型不等式并推广至多变量情形，且保持常数最佳性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a new version of one dimensional generalized discrete dual $p$-Hardy inequality with a shift. Using this generalized discrete dual p-Hardy inequality, we obtain improvements of two discrete dual $p$-Hardy inequalities. To be specific, for $p>1$ and $A\in C_c(\mathbb{N}_{0})$ satisfying $A_{0}=A_{1}=0$, we first improve the discrete dual p-Hardy inequality \begin{align*} &\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(n-1)^{p}| A_{n}-A_{n-1}|^{p}\geq\frac{1}{p^{p}}\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|A_{n}|^{p}, \end{align*} where the associate constant term is sharp. Subsequently, we improve its power-type weighted discrete dual p-Hardy extension \begin{align*} &\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(n-1)^α|A_{n}-A_{n-1}|^{p}\geq\Big(\frac{α+1-p}{p}\Big)^{p} \displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{|A_{n}|^{p}}{n^{p-α}} \end{align*} for $p-1<α\leq p$, where the associated constant term is also sharp. We also establish a discrete $p$- Birman inequality with power weights. Furthermore, we establish a multivariable dual $p$-Hardy inequality with a sharp constant. The proof proceeds by first establishing the inequality for two variables and then extending the argument to multiple variables, while preserving the sharpness of the constant.

</details>


### [112] [Upper bound properties of free and related Banach lattices via operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11928)
*Anastasiia Ianina,Timur Oikhberg*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入一种与映入 $X$ 的算子相关的新工具，研究 Banach 空间 $X$ 上自由 $p$-凸 Banach 格 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 及其相关格的 Fatou 性质、单调有界性、嵌入正则性，并揭示其与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由 $p$-凸 Banach 格可表示为单位球 $B_{X^*}$ 上的函数空间，由此产生若干更大的相关格。为了研究这些格的序结构性质（如上确界、向上有向集的上界等），需要新的工具。

Method: 引入一种与映入 $X$ 的算子相关的新工具，用于刻画 $B_{X^*}$ 上的函数格结构。

Result: 利用该工具：(i) 判定相关格是否具有涉及向上有向集上界的性质——即 Fatou 性质及相关的单调有界性；(ii) 研究相关空间之间嵌入的正则性。发现 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 的类 Fatou 性质与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质之间的联系。此外，给出 $X \subset Y$ 的例子，使得 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 不是 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$ 的正则子格。

Conclusion: ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 的 Fatou 型性质与 $X$ 的 Radon-Nikodym 性质存在联系；嵌入的正则性一般不成立，即存在 $X \subset Y$ 使 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ 非 ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$ 的正则子格。

Abstract: It is known that the free $p$-convex Banach lattice on a Banach space $X$ can be represented as a space of functions on the unit ball of $X^*$. In this way, it gives rise to certain related (larger) lattices. To investigate such lattices, we introduce a new tool, related to operators into $X$. This tool is then used to (i) determine whether the lattices in question possess, or fail, properties involving upper bounds of upward directed sets -- namely, the Fatou property, and the related property of monotonic boundedness; (ii) investigate the regularity of embeddings between spaces in question. We find connections between the Fatou-like properties of ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ and the Radon-Nikodym property of $X$. In addition, we give an example of $X \subset Y$ such that ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[X]$ is not a regular sublattice of ${\textrm{FBL}}^{(p)}[Y]$.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Finite Hardy Phase Theory for Power Means: An exact analytic shooting family through the Carleman parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10598)
*Tianchi Huang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究幂平均 \(P_t\)（\(t<1\)）的有限 Hardy 常数 \(\Lambda_N(t)\)，引入解析参数 \(q=t/(1-t)\)，给出统一标量 shooting 映射，证明存在唯一实解析相位 \(\kappa(q)\)，使有限缺陷展开的三次系数为零，并给出统一的渐近展开与系数 \(A_2,A_3,A_4\) 的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负幂、几何平均与常规正指数 Hardy 不等式分别对应 \(t<0\)、\(t=0\) 与 \(0<t<1\)，此前这些情形往往被分开处理，缺乏统一框架；本文希望用一个解析参数族的 shooting 方法统一刻画有限 Hardy 常数及其渐近修正。

Method: 令 \(q=t/(1-t)\in(-1,\infty)\)，\(\lambda_N(q)=\Lambda_N(q/(1+q))\)，构造对所有三种 regime 通用的精确标量 shooting 映射；该映射在 \(q=0\) 的表观奇点可去，并精确对应 Carleman 问题。使用统一终端条件与具二次临界瓶颈的极限向量场 \(y_*(q)=1+q\)、\(\Lambda_*(q)=(1+q)^{(1+q)/q}\)，引入唯一实解析相位 \(\kappa(q)\) 使缺陷展开的三次系数消失，并进行相位归一化。

Result: 证明了在 \((-1,\infty)\) 的紧子集上，对任意固定 \(L\ge2\)，\[ \Lambda_*(q)-\lambda_N(q)=\sum_{j=2}^{L}\frac{A_j(q)}{(\log N+\kappa(q))^j}+O\!\left((\log N)^{-L-1}\right), \] 且 \(A_2=2\pi^2(1+q)\Lambda_*\)、\(A_3=0\)、\(A_4=-\frac{\pi^2}{3}(2q^2+5q+5)A_2\)。Carleman 参数处 \(A_2(0)=2e\pi^2\)、\(A_4(0)=-(10/3)e\pi^4\)，后者由局部留数计算独立验证。相位归一化后，第一个含非常数离散响应的系数为 \(A_5\)，阶为 \((\log N+\kappa(q))^{-5}\)。

Conclusion: 经典 Carleman 与正 Hardy 的首阶修正可由同一解析族投影得到；相位本身包含全局离散缺陷。该框架统一了幂平均有限 Hardy 常数的精细渐近，并给出高阶系数的可控结构。

Abstract: For the power mean $P_t$, $t<1$, let \[ Λ_N(t)=\sup_{x_k>0} \frac{\sum_{n=1}^N P_t(x_1,\ldots,x_n)}{\sum_{n=1}^N x_n} \] be its finite Hardy constant. Negative powers, the geometric mean, and the usual positive-exponent Hardy inequality correspond respectively to $t<0$, $t=0$, and $0<t<1$. With the analytic parameter $q=t/(1-t)\in(-1,\infty)$, and writing $λ_N(q)=Λ_N(q/(1+q))$, we derive one exact scalar shooting map for all three regimes; its apparent singularity at $q=0$ is removable and gives the finite Carleman problem exactly. The map has a common terminal condition and its limiting vector field has a quadratic critical bottleneck at \[ y_*(q)=1+q,\qquad Λ_*(q)=(1+q)^{(1+q)/q}. \] We prove that there is a unique real-analytic phase $κ(q)$ for which the finite-defect expansion has zero cubic coefficient and, uniformly for $q$ in compact subsets of $(-1,\infty)$, for every fixed $L\ge2$, \[ Λ_*(q)-λ_N(q) =\sum_{j=2}^{L}\frac{A_j(q)}{(\log N+κ(q))^j} +O\!\left((\log N)^{-L-1}\right). \] In this phase-normalized scale, \[ A_2=2π^2(1+q)Λ_*,\qquad A_3=0,\qquad A_4=-\frac{π^2}{3}(2q^2+5q+5)A_2. \] Thus the classical Carleman and positive-Hardy leading corrections are projections of a single analytic family. At the Carleman parameter we obtain $A_2(0)=2eπ^2$ and $A_4(0)=-(10/3)eπ^4$; the latter is also recovered by a direct local residue calculation. The phase itself contains the global discrete defect. After phase normalization, the first coefficient involving a nonconstant discrete response is $A_5$, at order $(\log N+κ(q))^{-5}$.

</details>


### [114] [Dimension-free estimates for full discrete maximal functions associated with Euclidean balls and spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10763)
*Mahdi Hormozi,Jakub Niksiński,Błażej Wróbel*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了离散Hardy-Littlewood极大算子（球）在$\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$上的无量纲有界性（$1<p<\infty$），并证明了离散球面极大算子在$d\geq 5,2\leq p<\infty$时的无量纲有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续极大算子的无量纲（与维数无关）估计是调和分析中的重要问题。E.M. Stein提出了离散情形下球极大算子是否具有无量纲界的问题。此外，Magyar-Stein-Wainger定理给出了离散球面极大算子的$\ell^p$有界性，但与维数相关，能否在$p\geq 2$时去掉维数依赖尚待解决。

Method: 核心新思想是将相关Fourier乘子近似为归一化离散Gaussian乘子及其平移的有限线性组合。通过精细的鞍点分析（saddle-point analysis），得到在频率上一致且系数一致有界的近似。

Result: 证明了离散球极大算子在$1<p<\infty$时对所有维数$d$的$\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$无量纲有界；证明了离散球面极大算子在$d\geq 5,2\leq p<\infty$时的无量纲有界。

Conclusion: 球极大算子的结果解决了Stein提出的问题；球面极大算子的结果在$p\geq 2$范围内给出Magyar-Stein-Wainger定理的无量纲加强。

Abstract: We prove that the full discrete Hardy-Littlewood maximal operator associated with Euclidean balls satisfies dimension-free bounds on $\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$ for every $1<p<\infty$. We also establish analogous dimension-free bounds for the full discrete spherical maximal operator when $d\geq 5$ and $2\leq p<\infty$. The main new idea is to approximate the relevant Fourier multipliers by finite linear combinations of normalized discrete Gaussian multipliers and their translates. We obtain these approximations uniformly in frequency and with uniformly bounded coefficients through a refined saddle-point analysis. The ball result resolves a question of E.M. Stein, while the spherical result gives, in the range $p\geq 2$, a dimension-free strengthening of the theorem of Magyar, Stein, and Wainger.

</details>


### [115] [On the log-concavity of the composite Bessel function $x^αJ_{ν}\left( βx^γ\right) $](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11158)
*O. Kounchev,H. Render*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究复合Bessel函数 $F(x)=x^α J_ν(βx^γ)$ 的 $v(F)=f'f'-f''f$ 正性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $v(f)$ 的正性可推出 $|f|$ 在无零点子区间上严格对数凹，因此给出复合Bessel函数满足该性质的参数条件具有理论与应用价值。

Method: 定义 $v(f)=f'f'-f''f$，针对复合Bessel函数 $F(x)=J_{α,β,γ,ν}(x)=x^α J_ν(βx^γ)$（$β,γ>0$，$α,ν\in\mathbb{R}$）推导 $v(F)>0$ 的判别条件。

Result: 给出了使 $v(F)>0$ 成立的参数判据，进而保证 $|F|$ 在无零点的各子区间上严格对数凹。

Conclusion: 通过参数条件刻画复合Bessel函数的 $v(F)$ 正性，为其严格对数凹性提供了充分条件。

Abstract: For a twice differentiable function $f:\left( a,b\right) \rightarrow \mathbb{R}$ define $v\left( f\right) =f^{\prime}f^{\prime}-f^{\prime\prime }f.$ It is well known that the positivity of $v\left( f\right) $ implies that the function $\left\vert f\right\vert $ is strictly log-concave on each subinterval which does not contain zeros of $f.$ In this paper we provide criteria for the positivity of $v\left( F\right) $ for the composite Bessel function $F\left( x\right) =J_{α,β,γ,ν}\left( x\right) :=x^αJ_ν\left( βx^γ\right) $ for positive numbers $β$ and $γ$ and real numbers $α$ and $ν.$

</details>


### [116] [Riesz capacity ratios with negative exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11186)
*Qiuling Fan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究负指数 Riesz 容量比的锐不等式，结合计算实验与严格分析，在一维证明平衡质量正性，在平面与三维刻画离散构型与球的容量比比较，并给出高维正则单形优于球的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负指数 Riesz 容量比的极值问题缺乏系统理解，且已有猜想多依赖数值证据。作者希望通过计算实验发现规律、检验猜想，并用严格分析证明关键情形，从而厘清离散构型（多边形、单形、有限点集）与连续体（圆盘、球）在不同维数与参数区间的极值行为。

Method: 结合计算实验与严格分析：一维情形证明 -1<p<0 时有限点集的平衡质量正性，为数值检验猜想提供基础；平面情形比较圆盘与正则多边形顶点集，数值追踪等值曲线交点，提出奇多边形平衡测度猜想并给部分证明；三维情形数值研究正则单形与显式五、六点构型的等值曲线交点；并证明对固定 p<-2<q<0，足够高维时正则单形的容量比大于球。附 Python 与 Mathematica 代码支持复现。

Result: 证明了 -1<p<0 时一维有限点集平衡质量的正性；发现平面中圆盘与正则多边形比较存在竞争者之间的级联转变，且优于圆盘的区域并非简单嵌套；数值给出奇多边形平衡测度的精确猜想并部分证明；三维中正则单形与五、六点构型等值曲线出现类似交点；证明对每个固定 p<-2<q<0，正则单形在所有足够大维数中容量比大于球。

Conclusion: 负指数 Riesz 容量比的极值结构随维数与指数区间显著变化：离散构型可能超越连续体，且优势区域不具简单嵌套性；高维极限下正则单形相对球具有系统性优势。计算实验与严格证明互补，所附代码可推动后续猜想验证与推广。

Abstract: We investigate sharp inequalities for ratios of Riesz capacities with negative exponents by combining computational experiments with rigorous analysis. For finite subsets of the line, we prove positivity of equilibrium masses when $-1<p<0$, enabling numerical tests of conjectured extremal ratios. In the plane, comparisons of the disk with regular polygon vertex sets reveal a cascade of transitions among the tested competitors and suggest a precise conjecture for the equilibrium measure of odd polygons, for which we give a partial proof. Numerical intersections of equality curves show that the regions where these sets outperform the disk are not simply nested. Similar numerical intersections occur in three dimensions between the regular-simplex equality curve and those of explicit five-point and six-point configurations. Motivated by the dimensional dependence of these comparisons, we prove that for each fixed $p<-2<q<0$, the regular simplex has a larger capacity ratio than the ball in all sufficiently large dimensions. Accompanying Python and Mathematica code supports reproduction and further testing of the conjectures.

</details>


### [117] [Shorter proof of dimension-free $L^p$ estimates for maximal Riesz transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11537)
*Maciej Kucharski,Mateusz Kwaśnicki*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文给出极大Riesz变换（任意阶）$L^p$估计的一个更简短、更直接的证明，该估计用相应的Riesz变换控制极大算子，且常数与欧氏空间维数$d$无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始结果由Mateu、Orobitg、Pérez与Verdera给出，但其常数依赖于维数；Kucharski、Wróbel与Zienkiewicz将其改进为无维数不等式。本文旨在提供一个更短、更直接的证明途径。

Method: 采用更简短且直接的证明方法，建立极大Riesz变换（任意阶）的$L^p$估计，并保证所得常数与$\mathbb R^d$的维数无关。

Result: 得到极大Riesz变换（任意阶）的$L^p$估计，其控制项为相应的Riesz变换，且常数独立于维数$d$。

Conclusion: 为已有无维数极大Riesz变换$L^p$估计提供了更为简洁直接的证明。

Abstract: We provide a shorter and more direct proof of $L^p$ estimates for maximal Riesz transforms (of an arbitrary order) in terms of the corresponding Riesz transforms, with a constant independent of the dimension of the Euclidean space $\mathbb R^d$. This result was originally proved by Mateu, Orobitg, Pérez and Verdera with a constant depending on the dimension, and improved to a dimension-free inequality by Kucharski, Wróbel and Zienkiewicz.

</details>


### [118] [Liouville regular Jordan curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11598)
*Tomohiro Asano,Yuichi Ike*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Liouville正则Jordan曲线类，给出两个判定准则：局部变差条件与共形映射下径向尾像长度一致消失，并结合已有结果推出任意相似类中内接矩形的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liouville正则Jordan曲线类（即"存在连续Legendre提升的Jordan曲线"）此前已由作者引入，但缺乏便于验证的几何判定准则，且其与内接矩形等经典几何问题之间的联系有待建立。

Method: 一是证明在每点邻域内满足某种变差条件的Jordan曲线属于该曲线类；二是证明若共形映射下径向尾的像长度一致消失，则Jordan区域边界为Liouville正则。

Result: 上述两个判定准则成立，并与作者先前的结果相结合，推出每个给定相似类中都存在内接矩形。

Conclusion: Liouville正则性可由局部变差条件或共形映射下径向尾像长度的一致消失来刻画，该框架为内接矩形存在性提供了新的统一证明途径。

Abstract: We investigate the class of Liouville regular Jordan curves, which was introduced in our previous paper under the phrase "Jordan curves that admit continuous Legendrian lifts". We prove that a Jordan curve satisfying certain variation condition in a neighborhood of each point is in this class. We also prove that the boundary of a Jordan domain is Liouville regular if the images of the radial tails under a conformal mapping have uniformly vanishing length. Together with our previous result, these criteria imply the existence of an inscribed rectangle in every prescribed similarity class.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [An Improved Bound for the Ovals Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10775)
*Durvudkhan Suragan*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究闭曲线曲率平方 Schrödinger 算子首特征值的最优下界（Ovals 问题），证明 $\lambda_\gamma \geq \frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\left(\frac{\Gamma(7/6)}{\Gamma(5/3)}\right)^3$，并给出改进的双态动力学 Lieb-Thirring 不等式及相应双特征值常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ovals 问题要求在所有长度固定为 $2\pi$ 的闭平面曲线中，最小化周期曲率 Schrödinger 算子 $-d^2/ds^2+\kappa(s)^2$ 的首特征值。该问题与曲率能量、等周型谱估计及 Lieb-Thirring 型不等式密切相关，但长期缺乏接近最优的显式下界，且已有方法在几何直观与常数精度上均有提升空间。

Method: 作者提出一种新的几何方法：首先由闭曲线条件导出一个卷积恒等式；随后结合沿切线方向上的投影平均以及关于对径弧上的尖锐 Poincaré 不等式，对 $\lambda_\gamma$ 进行下界估计。该方法有效利用了曲线闭合性所隐含的全局约束与几何对称性。

Result: 得到近最优下界 $\lambda_\gamma \geq \frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}\left(\frac{\Gamma(7/6)}{\Gamma(5/3)}\right)^3$。此外，作为应用，获得了改进的两态动力学 Lieb-Thirring 不等式及其对应的两特征值常数。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Ovals 问题提供了一个近尖锐的显式下界，并发展了一种基于卷积恒等式与投影平均的几何分析框架，同时推广到 Lieb-Thirring 型谱不等式，具有方法与应用上的双重意义。

Abstract: Let $γ\subset\mathbb R^{m},\,m\geq2,$ be a closed curve of length $2π$ with its curvature $κ$, parametrized by arc length, and let $λ_γ$ be the first eigenvalue of the periodic curvature Schrödinger operator $-d^2/d s^2+κ(s)^2$. We obtain \[
  λ_γ\geq \frac{\sqrtπ}{2}
  \left(\frac{Γ(7/6)}{Γ(5/3)}\right)^3. \] This is a near-sharp lower bound for the Ovals problem. Our proof introduces a new geometric approach. We derive a convolution identity from the closure condition and combine it with projection averaging over tangent directions and sharp Poincaré inequalities on antipodal arcs. As applications, we provide an improved two-state kinetic Lieb-Thirring inequality and the corresponding two-eigenvalue constant.

</details>


### [120] [Regular hyperbolic tilings have no $\ell^2$ eigenfunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11857)
*Asaf Nachmias*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明了双曲平面任意正则镶嵌的1-骨架邻接算子无非零平方可积特征函数，并推广到具有正则对偶的平面嵌入的无限连通正则图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲平面正则镶嵌所对应图的邻接算子谱性质，特别是其是否存在非零平方可积特征函数（即绝对连续谱/无点谱问题）。

Method: 利用双曲平面正则镶嵌的几何对称性，将邻接算子的特征函数方程转化为几何分析问题，证明其不存在非零平方可积解；再通过正则对偶的平面嵌入将结论推广。

Result: 双曲平面任意正则镶嵌的1-骨架邻接算子无非零平方可积特征函数；所有具有正则对偶的平面嵌入的无限连通正则图同样如此。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了一大类几何上自然的无限正则图的谱性质，表明其邻接算子具有纯绝对连续谱且无平方可积特征函数。

Abstract: We show that the adjacency operator of the $1$-skeleton of any regular tiling of the hyperbolic plane has no nonzero square-integrable eigenfunctions. As a consequence, the same holds for every infinite connected regular graph admitting a proper planar embedding with regular dual.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A New Sparse Algorithm for Polynomial GCD over Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10626)
*Qiao-Long Huang,Michael Monagan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的整数系数多项式最大公因式(GCD)算法，通过变量替换将多元多项式化为分离多项式，把多元GCD归约为单一一元GCD，并用Maple实现，验证其位复杂度对输入输出规模及次数界是多项式的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元多项式GCD的传统方法通常需要归约到多个一元GCD，导致复杂度较高。作者希望借助新的变量替换，把多元多项式化为分离形式，从而只需一次一元GCD归约，并获得多项式位复杂度并提升实用效率。

Method: 采用将多元多项式GCD归约为一元多项式GCD的标准思路；核心是一种新的变量替换，使多元多项式变为分离多项式，即主变量中的系数均为单项式，从而实现单次一元GCD归约；随后给出显式位复杂度分析，并在Maple中实现算法。

Result: 新算法的位复杂度对输入规模、输出规模及各个变量的次数界均为多项式；实现结果表明，对于高次数、多变量但GCD项数较少的多项式，该算法表现高效。

Conclusion: 该变量替换方法可将多元GCD高效归约为单一一元GCD，在理论上具有多项式位复杂度，在实践上对高次多变量稀疏GCD问题具有良好效率。

Abstract: We describe a new greatest common divisor (GCD) algorithm for polynomials with integer coefficients. The bit complexity of the new algorithm is polynomial in the input and output sizes and the individual degree bounds.Our algorithm follows the standard approach by reducing multivariate polynomial GCD to univariate polynomial GCD.
  Our algorithm reduces a multivariate polynomial GCD to a single univariate polynomial GCD. The main idea of our algorithm is a new variable substitution which reduces a multivariate polynomial to a separated one, that is, the coefficients in a main variable are all monomials. The explicit bit complexity is analyzed and we have implemented our algorithm in Maple. It is shown that our algorithm is efficient for polynomials with high degree, large number of variables, but small number of terms in GCD.

</details>


### [122] [Sums of distinct divisors of factorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10902)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，改进此前 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究把 $1$ 到 $N$ 的每个整数表示为 $N$ 的至多 $k$ 个不同因子之和所需的最小 $k$，即 $h(N)$，并针对 $N=n!$ 改进已有上界。

Method: 将 Tenenbaum–Yokota 的递减贪心构造与 Berend–Harmse 更精确的阶乘因子间隙估计相结合；在 $\sqrt{n!}$ 以下和以上分别计步，上段借助倒数因子处理，从而保留间隙指数的主系数。

Result: 证明 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，得到显式常数 $2\log2$。

Conclusion: 该上界显著优于 Tenenbaum–Yokota 引理 4 及 Yokota 1995 年背包笔记中 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 阶的界。

Abstract: For practical $N$ let $h(N)$ be the least $k$ such that every integer $1\le m\le N$ is a sum of at most $k$ distinct divisors of $N$. We prove $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$. This improves the bounds of order $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ established in Tenenbaum-Yokota's Lemma 4 and Yokota's 1995 knapsack note. We combine their decreasing greedy construction with the sharper factorial divisor-gap estimate of Berend-Harmse. Counting the steps separately below and above $\sqrt{n!}$, with the upper range handled through reciprocal divisors, retains the leading coefficient in the gap exponent and yields the explicit constant $2\log2$.

</details>


### [123] [Ordinary 3-Isogeny Graphs and Improvement of Supersingularity Testing for Twisted Hessian Curves over Prime Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11037)
*Yuji Hashimoto,Koji Nuida*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用扭黑塞曲线间的3-同源性质，刻画了$\mathbb{F}_p$上扭黑塞曲线的3-火山结构，并改进Sutherland超奇异判定算法及$j$-不变量的立方刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\ell$-同源图对普通椭圆曲线的结构（$\ell$-火山）是Sutherland超奇异判定算法的核心。本文旨在针对特定曲线模型（扭黑塞曲线）和特定条件深入理解3-火山结构，以优化判定算法。

Method: 通过分析扭黑塞曲线之间的3-同源性质，研究当$p\equiv2\pmod3$、$\ell=3$时，$\mathbb{F}_p$上扭黑塞曲线的3-火山位置；并利用$j$-不变量的立方性质进行刻画。

Result: 证明当$p\equiv2\pmod3$且$\ell=3$时，$\mathbb{F}_p$上每条普通扭黑塞曲线都位于3-火山的表面；给出针对此类曲线的Sutherland超奇异判定算法的改进版本；并证明对$\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$上扭黑塞曲线$H(a,d)$，其$j$-不变量不是立方当且仅当该曲线普通且位于3-火山底面。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了超奇异$j$-不变量为立方的已知结论，并借助扭黑塞曲线的3-同源性质改进了超奇异判定算法，深化了对3-火山结构的理解。

Abstract: For any primes $p \neq \ell$, $\ell$-isogeny graphs of ordinary elliptic curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ have a typical structure called $\ell$-volcanoes, and the structure is the core of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm for elliptic curves. In this paper, by exploiting the properties of $3$-isogenies between twisted Hessian curves, we show that when $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$ and $\ell = 3$, every ordinary twisted Hessian curve defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ lies on the surface of the $3$-volcano. As an application, we give an improved version of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm specialized to twisted Hessian curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$. We also give a generalization of the known fact that any supersingular $j$-invariant is a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$; we show that for any twisted Hessian curve $H(a,d)$ defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, its $j$-invariant is not a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ if and only if $H(a,d)$ is ordinary and lies on the floor of a $3$-volcano.

</details>


### [124] [Effective estimates for exponential sums with multiplicative coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11070)
*Nicolas Robles*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究乘性函数在圆法指数和中的上界估计，改进了Bachman关于第二项中根号对数因子的结果，并证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montgomery与Vaughan已给出乘性函数指数和的经典上界，Bachman在1-有界函数情形下将第二项改进为NR^{-1/2}√(log R log log R)。本文旨在去掉Bachman第二项中的√(log R)因子，同时保留Montgomery-Vaughan原始系数假设，并探明平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Method: 证明结合了短区间上的Brun-Titchmarsh不等式与由Carleson-Hunt定理导出的极大Fourier估计；局部界允许任意依赖于素数的前缀。

Result: 在|f(p)|≤A、∑_{n≤x}|f(n)|²≤A²x、|α−a/q|≤q^{-2}、(a,q)=1、3≤R≤q≤N/R的条件下，证明 ∑_{n≤N}f(n)e(nα) ≪_A N/log N + (N/√R)√(log log(3R))，隐含常数有效；更精确的估计记录了与有理数的距离。

Conclusion: 去掉了Bachman第二项中的√(log R)因子，并在保留Montgomery-Vaughan系数假设下得到改进，同时证明了平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Abstract: Let $f$ be multiplicative, with $|f(p)|\le A$ at primes and $\sum_{n\le x}|f(n)|^2\le A^2x$ for every $x\ge1$. If $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $3\le R\le q\le N/R$, we prove \[
  \sum_{n\le N}f(n)\operatorname{e}(nα)
  \ll_A \frac{N}{\log N}
  +\frac{N}{\sqrt R}\sqrt{\log\log(3R)} \] with effective implied constants. Montgomery and Vaughan proved this with second term $NR^{-1/2}(\log R)^{3/2}$, and, for $1$-bounded functions, Bachman replaced it by $NR^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log R\log\log R}$. We remove the factor $\sqrt{\log R}$ from Bachman's second term while retaining the original coefficient hypotheses of Montgomery and Vaughan. A more precise estimate records the distance from a rational number. The proof combines the Brun-Titchmarsh inequality on short intervals with maximal Fourier estimates derived from the Carleson-Hunt theorem; the local bounds permit arbitrary prime-dependent prefixes. We also prove sharpness of the square-root displacement dependence.

</details>


### [125] [Rational Approximations for Reciprocals of Multiple Zeta Values and Trivariate Cauchy Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11072)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，研究第一类与第二类Cauchy数（Gregory系数与Nörlund数）的三变量推广，给出高阶Gregory系数零点、最终交错性、最终正性等结果，并导出zeta值与双zeta值的倒数积分恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Cauchy数（第一类Gregory系数、第二类Nörlund数）均为非零且具有最终交错性等性质。作者希望借助多重对数函数倒数的高阶Laurent展开，将这两类数推广到三变量、任意阶（order）的情形，并考察哪些经典性质在推广后仍然成立、哪些会发生变化。

Method: 对多重对数函数的正整数次幂（阶ℓ）之倒数作Laurent展开，据此定义三变量推广的Cauchy数；对对数情形用WZ方法证明高阶Gregory系数存在零点；通过数值证据提出最终正性猜想，并在对数、单重及双重对数等特殊情形下予以验证；随后利用对数与多重对数关系导出zeta值、双zeta值的倒数恒等式。

Result: 在对数情形下，对每个阶ℓ>1，至少有一个ℓ阶Gregory系数为零，这与所有经典Gregory系数非零形成对比；同时证明对每个固定阶，该序列最终交替，保持了经典Gregory系数的性质。在最一般情形下，数值证据强烈支持“序列最终为正”的猜想，并在单重对数和双重对数特殊情形中证明了该猜想。此外，对每个zeta值和双zeta值，得到一无穷族恒等式，将其倒数表示为有理数与一个瑕积分之和。

Conclusion: 多重对数倒数的高阶Laurent展开为Cauchy数提供了一个统一的三变量推广框架：经典的非零性与最终交错性在高阶对数情形下分别被破坏与保留，而最终正性猜想在低重对数情形成立并被数值证据支持；由此还派生出zeta值与双zeta值的倒数积分恒等式。

Abstract: In this paper, we will study a trivariate extension of the Cauchy numbers of both the first kind (also called Gregory coefficients) and the second kind (also called Nörlund numbers) via the Laurent expansion of the reciprocal of any positive integer power (which is called the order) of multiple polylogarithms. In the case of logarithm, we will show by the WZ method that for each order $\ell>1$ some Gregory coefficient of order $\ell$ must vanish, in contrast to the fact that all classical Gregory coefficients are nonzero. We also prove in this higher order logarithm case that the sequence is eventually alternating for each fixed order, a property enjoyed by the classical Gregory coefficients. In the most general setting, we conjecture that these new sequences are all eventually positive, which is supported by strong numerical evidence. Finally, we confirm this conjecture in the special case of polylogarithms and double polylogarithms. As a by product, for each zeta value and double zeta value, we find an infinite family of identities expressing its reciprocal as a sum of a rational number and an improper integral.

</details>


### [126] [A $p$-adic monodromy theorem for curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11106)
*Hansheng Diao,Yong Suk Moon,Zijian Yao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明光滑射影曲线上的 de Rham p 进局部系统经有限覆盖后变为半稳定，建立经典 p 进单值定理的相对版本，并附带证明圆盘与圆环上的对应定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berger 以及 André–Kedlaya–Mebkhout 的经典 p 进单值定理（monodromy theorem）刻画了单个局部域上 de Rham 表示的性质，但缺少曲线（相对情形）上的推广。本文旨在将这一刚性结论相对化到 p 进域上的光滑射影曲线，回答 de Rham p 进局部系统在几何方向上的单值行为问题。

Method: 技术路线是相对化的 p 进单值理论：先在刚性解析几何中的圆盘与圆环上证明 de Rham p 进局部系统的 p 进单值定理，再将局部结果沿曲线拼接，通过对曲线取合适的有限覆盖（pullback）把整体系统归约到局部情形。

Result: 证明任一 P^1 型光滑射影曲线上的 de Rham p 进局部系统都是潜在半稳定（potentially semistable）的，即沿某有限覆盖拉回后变为半稳定；同时得到圆盘与圆环上 de Rham p 进局部系统的 p 进单值定理。

Conclusion: 本文把 Berger 与 André–Kedlaya–Mebkhout 的经典 p 进单值定理推广为相对版本，确立了曲线情形下 de Rham 局部系统的潜在半稳定性，构成 p 进局部系统单值理论的相对/几何推广。

Abstract: We prove that every de Rham $p$-adic local system on a smooth projective curve over a $p$-adic field is potentially semistable; that is, it becomes semistable after pulling back along a finite cover of the curve. This establishes a relative version of the classical $p$-adic monodromy theorem of Berger and André--Kedlaya--Mebkhout. Along the way, we also establish a $p$-adic monodromy theorem for de Rham $p$-adic local systems on disks and annuli.

</details>


### [127] [Moments and Non-Vanishing of Maass Form Symmetric Square L-Functions in Short Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11119)
*Olga Balkanova,Dmitry Frolenkov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Maass尖形式对称平方L-函数中心值三次矩的均值Lindelöf估计的短区间长度门槛，从H≥T^{18/19+ε}改进为H≫T^{6/7+ε}，并由此无条件下改进了短区间内中心L-值非零比例的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li此前在短区间(T-H, T+H)（长度H≥T^{18/19+ε}）上得到了Maass形式对称平方L-函数中心值三次矩的均值Lindelöf估计。作者希望缩短所需区间长度，并在更短区间上获得中心L-值非零比例更好的下界，突破此前仅在Dirichlet L-函数Lindelöf假设下才得到的（3β-1）/4这一结果。

Method: 证明的关键是一个关于Maass对称平方L-函数第二扭转矩的新渐近公式；基于该公式推导短区间上的均值Lindelöf估计，并进一步用于估计中心L-值非零比例的下界。

Result: 将均值Lindelöf估计成立的区间长度条件从H≥T^{18/19+ε}改进到H≫T^{6/7+ε}；并给出非零比例的无条件下界（7β-2）/8，优于此前即使在Dirichlet L-函数Lindelöf假设下仍只能得到的（3β-1）/4。

Conclusion: 通过新的第二扭转矩渐近公式，作者在更短的区间上建立了对称平方L-函数中心值三次矩的均值Lindelöf估计，并显著提升了短区间内中心L-值非零比例的下界。

Abstract: Recently, Li obtained a mean Lindelöf estimate for the cubic moment of the central values of Maass form symmetric square $L$-functions over short intervals $(T-H, T+H)$ of length $H \ge T^{18/19+ε}$. We improve this result by showing that the estimate holds for $H \gg T^{6/7+ε}$. The key ingredient in our proof is a new asymptotic formula for the second twisted moment of Maass symmetric square $L$-functions. Based on this formula, we also improve the lower bound for the proportion of non-vanishing central $L$-values in short intervals. Previously, even under the assumption of the Lindelöf hypothesis for Dirichlet $L$-functions, the proportion of non-vanishing values in intervals of length $H = T^β$ was only known to be at least $\frac{3β-1}{4}$. We establish an unconditional lower bound of $\frac{7β-2}{8}$.

</details>


### [128] [Log-crystalline representations and $(\varphi, Γ)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11266)
*Abhinandan,Denis Benois*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究混合特征局部域上小仿射代数R的log-étale基本群G_R的p进表示，用étale (φ, Γ_R)-模对其进行分类，并定义log-crystalline表示，证明其与R上的Wach模等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将晶体表示理论从非对数（光滑）情形推广到对数情形，把此前关于晶体情形的Wach模等价结果延伸到对数设定，为混合特征局部域上的p进表示提供统一的分类框架。

Method: 设F为绝对非分歧混合特征局部域，R为O_F上的小仿射代数；取一般纤维上的log-étale基本群G_R（Fujiwara–Kato意义下带"水平"log结构）；研究其p进表示，并用étale (φ, Γ_R)-模建立分类；进而定义log-crystalline表示并构造对应的Wach模。

Result: 给出了G_R的p进表示借助étale (φ, Γ_R)-模的分类；定义了log-crystalline表示这一非对数/光滑晶体表示的推广；证明G_R的log-crystalline表示与R上的Wach模等价。

Conclusion: 对数设定下的p进表示理论可由étale (φ, Γ_R)-模与Wach模刻画，log-crystalline表示与Wach模的等价推广了此前的晶体情形结果。

Abstract: Let $F$ be an absolutely unramified mixed characteristic local field with rings of integers $O_F$. For a small affine algebra $R$ over $O_F$, we consider the logarithmic étale fundamental group $G_R$ of its generic fibre equipped with a "horizontal" log-structure, in the sense of Fujiwara--Kato. We study $p$-adic representations of $G_R$ and obtain a classification of these representations in terms of étale $(\varphi, Γ_R)$-modules. Moreover, we define and study the notion of log-crystalline representations of $G_R$, a generalisation of crystalline representations from the non-logarithmic/smooth case. Furthermore, in the logarithmic setting, we show that log-crystalline representations of $G_R$ are equivalent to Wach modules for $R$, extending our previous results from the (non-logarithmic) crystalline case.

</details>


### [129] [The irrationality measure of π is at most 7.101862832357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11276)
*Yufei Bai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立的分子指数并取特定参数，改进了π的无理性测度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Zeilberger–Zudilin方法给出的π无理性测度上界，探索更优参数点。

Method: 在Zeilberger–Zudilin积分中引入两个独立的分子指数A₁、A₂，并将其特化为A₁=A₂=1857/2785，构造关于1和π的整系数线性型；同时分析辅助上界函数在该参数点处的局部性质。

Result: 得到μ(π)<7.101862832357，较Zeilberger–Zudilin上界7.103205334137…降低超过0.001342501780（未取整差值约0.01890%）；该参数点是辅助上界函数在其容许算术区域内的严格二维局部极小点。

Conclusion: 该工作改进了π无理性测度的已知上界，并给出参数点的局部最优性结论；但仅为该函数的局部性质，并不声称其为所有构造下的全局最优。

Abstract: We introduce two independent numerator exponents into the Zeilberger--Zudilin integral and specialize them to \[
  A_1=A_2=\frac{1857}{2785}. \] The resulting integer linear forms in $1$ and $π$ prove \[
  μ(π)<7.101862832357. \] This lowers the Zeilberger--Zudilin upper bound $7.103205334137\ldots$ by more than $0.001342501780$; the difference between the unrounded bounds is $0.0013425017806509\ldots$, approximately $0.01890\%$. The same parameter point is a strict two-dimensional local minimizer of the explicit auxiliary upper-bound function in its admissible arithmetic chamber. This is a local statement about that function, not a claim that the point is a global optimizer among all constructions.

</details>


### [130] [On the bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture in equal characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11456)
*Yufan Luo,Yiqi Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究等特征函数域上模p伽罗瓦表示的有界导子有限性猜想：证明在存在处处非分歧特征零提升的条件性有限性定理，并构造反例表明去掉该提升假设后猜想一般不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察Moon–Taguchi有界导子有限性猜想在等特征（特征p全局函数域）情形下的成立范围，厘清"存在特征零提升"这一假设是否为猜想成立的必要条件，并解决Moon与Taguchi提出的二维情形问题。

Method: 在特征p全局函数域K上研究连续、半单、处处非分歧、几何的表示ρ: G_K → GL_n(F̄_p)。先证明附加"存在处处非分歧特征零提升"假设下的条件性有限性定理；再通过构造无穷多个函数域K及满射绝对不可约表示ρ_r: G_K ↠ SL_2(F_{p^r})（r ≥ 4）来推翻去掉提升假设后的猜想，并据此构造正则于F_p、伽罗瓦群为∏_{r≥4} PSL_2(F_{p^r})的处处非分歧扩张，结合已知跨特征有限性定理完成二维情形的判定。

Result: 得到条件性有限性：固定K与n时，满足提升假设的表示仅有有限多个同构类；构造出无穷多个特征p函数域及对应满射绝对不可约几何表示ρ_r: G_K ↠ SL_2(F_{p^r})（r≥4），表明猜想在无提升假设时失败；作为推论得到处处非分歧、正则于F_p、伽罗瓦群为∏_{r≥4} PSL_2(F_{p^r})的伽罗瓦扩张L/K。

Conclusion: Moon–Taguchi猜想在等特征情形需以"存在处处非分歧特征零提升"为条件方成立；去掉该假设则不成立。二维情形被完全解决：此类处处非分歧扩张在特征p的全局函数域上存在，而在特征不等于p的全局函数域上不存在。

Abstract: We investigate the equal-characteristic case of the Moon--Taguchi bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture for mod $p$ Galois representations over global function fields. We first establish a conditional finiteness theorem: for any global function field $K$ of characteristic $p$ and any integer $n \ge 1$, there are only finitely many isomorphism classes of continuous, semisimple, everywhere unramified, and geometric representations $ρ: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ that admit an everywhere unramified characteristic-zero lift. Furthermore, we prove that the conjecture fails in general without this lifting hypothesis. Concretely, we construct infinitely many global function fields $K$ of characteristic $p$ admitting a continuous, surjective, absolutely irreducible, everywhere unramified, and geometric representation $ρ_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$ for each integer $r \ge 4$. As a corollary, we construct an everywhere unramified Galois extension \(L/K\), regular over \(\mathbf F_p\), with Galois group
  \[
  \mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong
  \prod_{r\geq4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf F_{p^r}).
  \]
  Combined with known cross-characteristic finiteness theorems, this completely resolves the question posed by Moon and Taguchi in dimension two: such extensions exist over global function fields of characteristic \(p\), whereas they cannot exist over global function fields of characteristic different from \(p\).

</details>


### [131] [Sharp unconditional moment bounds for products of L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11619)
*Markus Valås Hagen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究临界线上不可约 $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ 与 $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-函数乘积的混合矩，建立了尖锐的矩界，并在一定范围内确定了其阶。特别地，首次无条件确定了负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级。下界证明引入了一种新的混合矩计算方法，融合了 Heath--Brown 经典方法与 Harper、Heap、Radziwill、Soundararajan 的现代技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ 与 $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-函数乘积混合矩的阶，特别是无条件确定负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级，此问题此前未有定论。

Method: 结合 Heath--Brown 的经典方法与 Harper、Heap、Radziwill、Soundararajan 的现代机器，提出一种新的混合矩计算方法用于下界证明；上界部分建立尖锐矩界。

Result: 建立了混合矩的尖锐界，在一定范围内确定了其阶；首次无条件确定负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩的量级。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了 $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ 与 $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-函数乘积混合矩的阶，并解决了负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩量级这一长期问题，其新方法具有独立价值。

Abstract: We investigate the mixed moments of products of irreducible $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-functions on the critical line, and establish sharp moment bounds for these quantities. In particular this establishes the order of these mixed moments in certain ranges. This determines unconditionally, for the first time, the order of magnitude of the first moment of cubic Dedekind zeta functions with negative discriminant. The proof of the lower bound introduces a new method for computing mixed moments, that is a mix of the classical method of Heath--Brown with the modern machinery of Harper, Heap, Radziwill, and Soundararajan.

</details>


### [132] [Internal congruences modulo powers of $2$ for overpartition tuples with odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11806)
*Manjil P. Saikia,Prabal Talukdar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了奇部overpartition $m$-元组的生成函数在$U$-算子迭代下满足模 $2^{i+2}$ 的同余式，从而给出 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$，并指出2-adic赋值相等当且仅当 $n$ 为奇数平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇部overpartition $m$-元组计数的2-adic性质（同余与2-adic赋值分布）是分拆同余理论的自然问题；已有结果多针对特定 $m$ 或依赖复杂工具，作者希望给出对所有奇数 $m$ 一致、且初等的证明框架。

Method: 方法为初等且对 $m$ 一致：构造由三项递推给出的单族整系数多项式，用以统一刻画所有涉及的 $U$-算子恒等式；并通过一个整除性命题，在每次迭代中提供一个因子 $2$，从而逐步提升模幂次。

Result: 对每个奇数 $m\ge1$ 与每个 $i\ge3$，生成函数满足 $\sum_{n\ge0}(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n))q^n\equiv 2^{i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2}\pmod{2^{i+2}}$；由此 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$，且2-adic赋值恰在奇数平方处取等。

Conclusion: 结果给出奇部overpartition $m$-元组计数在二进制尺度变换下的统一同余与2-adic赋值刻画，表明其奇异性完全由奇数平方项控制；证明的初等性与对 $m$ 的一致性提示该递推多项式方法可推广至其他受 $U$-算子支配的分拆型同余问题。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ denote the number of overpartition $m$-tuples of $n$ into odd parts. We prove that for every odd $m\ge1$ and every $i\ge3$, \[\sum_{n\ge0}\Bigl(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^in\bigr)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^{i-1}n\bigr)\Bigr)q^n \equiv 2^{\,i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2} \pmod{2^{\,i+2}} .\] Thus $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv \overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$, with equality of $2$-adic valuations exactly at the odd squares. The proof is elementary and uniform in $m$: a single family of integer polynomials, given by a three-term recurrence, governs every $U$-operator identity involved, and a divisibility statement supplies one power of $2$ per iteration.

</details>


### [133] [Generalized Frobenius Partitions Modulo Powers of $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11813)
*Manjil P. Saikia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了 k-色广义 Frobenius 分拆函数 cφ_k(n) 在模 2^m 下的同余关系式，并应用于 k=18 和 k=10 的情形，完整刻画了 cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16 及 cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8，证明了 Das–Nath–Sarma 近期猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-色广义 Frobenius 分拆函数的同余性质是分拆理论的重要课题；已有 Chan–Wang–Yang 的 m=2 同余结果和 Das–Nath–Sarma (2026) 的猜想，需要更一般的同余框架来统一并推广这些结果。

Method: 利用经典 theta 函数 φ(q) 建立对任意 m≥2 及 k≡2 (mod 2^m) 的模 2^m 同余生成函数恒等式；对 m=2 退化为已有结果；将 k=18、k=10 代入并结合生成函数变换与模运算进行分析。

Result: 证明了主同余式；k=18 时完整确定 cφ_18(2n+1) mod 16，并给出 Σ cφ_18(6n+1)q^n ≡ 4Σ q^{r(3r-1)/2} (mod 16)；由此证明 cφ_18(30n+19) ≡ cφ_18(30n+25) ≡ 0 (mod 16) 的猜想，并得到更多 mod 16 同余及简洁的 mod 8 刻画；k=10 时确定了 cφ_10(2n+1) mod 8。

Conclusion: 建立了一类一般性的模 2^m 同余框架，统一并推广了先前结果，证明并扩展了 Das–Nath–Sarma 的猜想，展示了该方法的有效性与可推广性。

Abstract: Let $cφ_k(n)$ denote the number of $k$-colored generalized Frobenius partitions of $n$. We prove that, for every $m\geq2$ and every $k\equiv2\pmod{2^m}$, \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_k(n)q^n\equiv\frac{\varphi(q)\,(q^2;q^2)_\infty}{(q;q)_\infty^2}\sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}\pmod{2^m}, \] where $\varphi(q)$ is the classical theta function. For $m=2$ this recovers a congruence of Chan, Wang, and Yang. Applied with $k=18$, we determine $cφ_{18}(2n+1)$ modulo $16$ completely. In particular, we also prove \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{18}(6n+1)q^n
  \equiv4\sum_{r\in\mathbb{Z}}q^{r(3r-1)/2}\pmod{16}, \] which proves the congruences $cφ_{18}(30n+19)\equiv cφ_{18}(30n+25)\equiv0\pmod{16}$ recently conjectured by Das, Nath, and Sarma (2026). It also yields further congruences modulo $16$ and a simple modulo-$8$ characterization that recovers and extends a recent congruence of those authors. As a second application we set $k=10$ and determine $cφ_{10}(2n+1)$ modulo $8$.

</details>


### [134] [Bounded asymptotic bases for linear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11848)
*Christian Táfula*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究线性型 b₁x₁+…+b_h x_h 的有界表示集，证明对任意非 (1,1) 的二元 b 及若干高维族（如 (u₁, p^d u₂, …, p^{(h-1)d}u_h)，p∤∏uᵢ）均存在这样的集合 A⊆ℕ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加性数论中带系数线性型的有界表示问题，推广经典基与表示函数理论，探讨在何种系数条件下存在使每个充分大整数具有有界正表示数的集合 A。

Method: 构造性方法：对满足 gcd(b₁,…,b_h)=1 的正整数向量 b，构造 A⊆ℕ，使充分大整数的表示数有界且为正；重点处理二元 b≠(1,1) 情形，并推广到含素数幂的高维族。

Result: 证明了对每个二元向量 b≠(1,1) 都存在所需集合 A；并对包括 b=(u₁, p^d u₂, …, p^{(h-1)d}u_h)（p∤u₁⋯u_h）在内的一般高维族也证明了存在性。

Conclusion: 带系数线性型的有界表示集在广泛系数条件下存在，二元情形除 (1,1) 外全部成立，高维情形的构造具有推广性，为后续研究更一般向量 b 提供了框架。

Abstract: For a vector of positive integers $\mathbf{b} = (b_1,\ldots,b_h)$ with $\gcd(b_1,\ldots,b_h) = 1$, we study sets $A \subseteq \mathbb{N}$ for which every sufficiently large integer has a bounded positive number of representations \[ n = b_1 x_1 + \cdots + b_h x_h \qquad (x_1,\ldots,x_h\in A). \] We prove that such a set exists for every binary vector $\mathbf{b} \neq (1,1)$, and for some general higher-dimensional families, including $\mathbf{b} = (u_1, p^d u_2, \ldots, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)$ where $p\nmid u_1\cdots u_h$.

</details>


### [135] [Chowla's non-vanishing conjecture over $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11855)
*Peter Koymans,Carlo Pagano,Mark Shusterman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明在函数域 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ 上，当 $q\equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 时，100% 的虚二次 Dirichlet 特征在其中心点 $\tfrac{1}{2}$ 处取值非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注 Dirichlet $L$-函数在中心点的非零性问题，即对非零比例（此处为 100%）的虚二次特征证明 $L(\tfrac{1}{2},\chi)\neq 0$。这属于解析数论中 $L$-函数中心值非消没的经典问题，在函数域情形下寻求定量且最优的结论。

Method: 在函数域 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$（$q\equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 为素数幂）的框架下，研究虚二次 Dirichlet 特征族，并通过解析手段（如平均估计、特征族的矩方法等）建立中心点非零性的比例结论。

Result: 证明了对于 100% 的 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ 上的虚二次 Dirichlet 特征 $\chi$，均有 $L(\tfrac{1}{2},\chi)\neq 0$。

Conclusion: 在 $q\equiv 1 \bmod 8$ 的条件下，虚二次 Dirichlet 特征的中心 $L$-值几乎必然非零，即非零比例达到 100%，给出了函数域情形下的最优非消没结果。

Abstract: Let $q \equiv 1 \bmod 8$ be a prime power. We prove that $L\left(\tfrac{1}{2}, χ\right) \neq 0$ for 100% of the imaginary quadratic Dirichlet characters of $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$.

</details>


### [136] [The asymptotic in Waring's problem over function fields beyond twice the degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11888)
*Matthew Hase-Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上的 Waring 问题中，当 $n>2d$、特征大于 $(d-1)^2$ 且 $q$ 满足显式下界时，证明了带有幂次节省误差项的主渐近估计；该范围对目标多项式一致的期望渐近而言一般是尖锐的。核心技术贡献是按奇异轨迹余维数对泛函分类的聚合小弧估计，并借助相交理论控制参数空间的次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上 Waring 问题的主渐近估计及其误差项，目标是给出带幂次节省的渐近公式，并确定使该渐近一致成立的条件范围（$n$ 与 $d$ 的关系、特征下限、$q$ 的显式下界）。作者指出该范围对目标多项式一致的期望渐近而言一般是尖锐的，因此动机在于将已有的期望渐近结果推进到接近最优的参数范围。

Method: 主要新工具是聚合小弧估计：按泛函所关联奇异轨迹的余维数对泛函进行分类，再使用相交理论来界定由此产生的参数空间的次数。通过这种按余维数的分层归类，将众多小弧贡献聚合处理，从而获得所需的幂次节省误差。当 $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$ 时，所需的 $q$ 下界可表示为关于 $d$ 的多项式，次数为 $2+4/\varepsilon$。

Result: 在 $n>2d$、特征大于 $(d-1)^2$、且 $q$ 满足显式下界时，证明了 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上 Waring 问题的期望渐近，并带有幂次节省误差项。该参数范围对于一致于目标多项式的期望渐近而言一般是尖锐的。特别地，在 $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$ 情形下，$q$ 的下界为关于 $d$ 的多项式，次数为 $2+4/\varepsilon$。

Conclusion: 论文给出了函数域 Waring 问题中带幂次节省误差的期望渐近，条件为 $n>2d$、特征大于 $(d-1)^2$ 及 $q$ 的显式下界，并说明该范围一般是尖锐的。其关键在于按奇异轨迹余维数分类泛函并结合相交理论控制参数空间次数的聚合小弧估计；在 $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$ 时，$q$ 的下界是关于 $d$ 的 $2+4/\varepsilon$ 次多项式。

Abstract: We prove the expected asymptotic in Waring's problem over $\mathbb F_q[T]$, with a power-saving error, whenever $n>2d$, the characteristic is greater than $(d-1)^2$, and $q$ satisfies an explicit lower bound. This range is sharp in general for the expected asymptotic uniformly in the target polynomial.
  Our main new input is an aggregate minor arc estimate: we count functionals according to the codimension of their associated singular loci and use intersection theory to bound the degrees of the resulting parameter spaces. In particular, if $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$, the required lower bound on $q$ is polynomial in $d$ of degree $2+4/\varepsilon$.

</details>

### [137] [Nonamenable groups whose reduced group C*-algebras are not pure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10653)
*Jamie Bell*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明了非顺从群 (ℤ/2ℤ)≀Γ 的约化群 C*-代数含与 K(ℓ²(Γ)) 同构的理想-商，从而非纯，否定 Thiel 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Thiel 关于约化群 C*-代数是否纯（pure）的问题，构造反例以给出否定回答。

Method: 对任意可数无限离散群 Γ，考察限制圈积 (ℤ/2ℤ)≀Γ 的约化群 C*-代数，构造出同构于 K(ℓ²(Γ)) 的理想-商（ideal-quotient）。

Result: 该约化群 C*-代数不是 nowhere scattered，因而非纯；取 Γ 非顺从即得非顺从群的反例。

Conclusion: 给出 Thiel 问题（约化群 C*-代数的纯性）的否定答案，表明非顺从群的约化群 C*-代数可以非纯。

Abstract: We exhibit nonamenable groups whose reduced group C*-algebras are not pure. More precisely, if $Γ$ is any countably infinite discrete group, then the reduced group C*-algebra of the restricted wreath product $(\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}) \wr Γ$ has an ideal-quotient isomorphic to $\mathcal{K}(\ell^2(Γ))$. It is therefore not nowhere scattered and, in particular, not pure. Taking $Γ$ nonamenable gives a negative answer to a question of Thiel concerning pureness of reduced group C*-algebras.

</details>


### [138] [Von Neumann algebras as reduced twisted groupoid $C^*$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10835)
*Alcides Buss,Luiz Felipe Garcia,Tomás Pacheco*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了作为C*-代数同构于带满支撑连续Haar系统的局部紧Hausdorff群胚的约化扭C*-代数的von Neumann代数，它们恰为次齐性von Neumann代数（等价于阿贝尔von Neumann代数上矩阵代数的有限乘积）；同时给出单位元判定准则，并引入控制传播性质证明B(H)与Calkin代数不能嵌入此类约化或Borel群胚代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望明确哪些von Neumann代数可以由群胚约化扭C*-代数实现，并给出相应群胚的规范形式；同时寻求将一般Haar系统下的分类问题化归到étale情形，并寻找阻止非有限von Neumann代数（如B(H)、Calkin代数）嵌入群胚代数的内在障碍。

Method: 通过对带满支撑连续Haar系统的局部紧Hausdorff群胚的约化扭C*-代数进行C*-代数同构刻画，证明其与次齐性von Neumann代数一致，并构造可取为紧、主、étale、平凡扭、计数Haar系统的群胚；利用单位元条件证明一般Haar系统的分类可化归étale情形；定义控制传播性质，借助到一致稀疏矩阵范数闭包的忠实表示进行研究，并考察约化扭étale群胚代数及其Borel完备的非Hausdorff情形。

Result: 此类von Neumann代数恰为次齐性von Neumann代数，即阿贝尔von Neumann代数上矩阵代数的有限乘积；群胚总可取为紧、主、étale且扭平凡、Haar系统为计数测度。对这类群胚，全或约化扭代数是单位的当且仅当群胚étale且单位空间紧。每个约化扭étale群胚代数及其Borel完备具有控制传播性质，包括单位空间局部紧Hausdorff的非Hausdorff群胚；对任意无限维Hilbert空间H，从B(H)的非零商到具控制传播代数的*-同态必为零，故B(H)与Calkin代数均不能嵌入这些约化或Borel群胚代数；具控制传播的von Neumann代数必为有限，且无需可分性或可数性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了由满支撑连续Haar系统群胚约化扭C*-代数得到的von Neumann代数类，给出单位元判定并把分类化归étale情形；引入的控制传播性质成为非有限von Neumann代数嵌入的刚性障碍，表明B(H)与Calkin代数不能嵌入相应约化或Borel群胚代数，且具该性质的von Neumann代数必有限。

Abstract: We characterize the von Neumann algebras that are isomorphic, as $C^*$-algebras, to reduced twisted $C^*$-algebras of locally compact Hausdorff groupoids equipped with continuous Haar systems of full support. They are exactly the subhomogeneous von Neumann algebras, equivalently finite products of matrix algebras over abelian von Neumann algebras. The groupoid can always be chosen compact, principal, and étale, with trivial twist and counting Haar system.
  We also prove that, for a groupoid in this class, the full or reduced twisted algebra is unital if and only if the groupoid is étale with compact unit space. This criterion reduces the classification for general Haar systems to the étale case.
  A further obstruction comes from controlled propagation, defined through faithful representations into the norm closure of uniformly sparse matrices. Every reduced twisted étale groupoid algebra and its Borel completion have controlled propagation, including for non-Hausdorff groupoids with locally compact Hausdorff unit space. For every infinite-dimensional Hilbert space $H$, every $*$-homomorphism from a nonzero quotient of $B(H)$ into an algebra with controlled propagation is zero. In particular, neither $B(H)$ nor the Calkin algebra embeds into any of these reduced or Borel groupoid algebras. We also prove that every von Neumann algebra with controlled propagation is finite. No separability or countability assumptions are required.

</details>


### [139] [On the II$_{1}$ Factors of Fuchsian Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11074)
*D. Shlyakhtenko*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 闭可定向曲面基本群的von Neumann代数同构于自由群因子，并由此解决de la Harpe–Voiculescu猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲面基本群与PSL₂(ℝ)中有限生成无挠非初等离散子群的von Neumann代数结构，判定其是否为自由群因子，并解决de la Harpe与Voiculescu提出的猜想。

Method: 证明自由群𝔽₂中元素w=ABA⁻¹B⁻¹在群因子中被自由补全，即L(𝔽₂)=W*(w)*W*(v)，其中v为与w自由独立的Haar酉元；结合已有结果推广至一般子群Γ⊂PSL₂(ℝ)。

Result: 亏格g≥2的闭可定向曲面基本群的von Neumann代数为2g−1个生成元上的自由群因子；任意有限生成无挠非初等离散子群Γ⊂PSL₂(ℝ)的L(Γ)为自由群因子。

Conclusion: 证实了de la Harpe–Voiculescu猜想，表明上述群因子的自由性；该结果由OpenAI的ChatGPT Pro 6.0获得。

Abstract: We show that von Neumann algebras of fundamental groups of closed orientable surfaces of genus $g\geq2$ are free group factors on $2g-1$generators. The key technical ingredient involves a proof that the element $w=ABA^{-1}B^{-1}$ of the free group $\mathbb{F}_{2}=\langle A,B\rangle$ is freely complemented in the group factor: $L(\mathbb{F}_{2})=W^{*}(w)*W^{*}(v)$ for some Haar unitary $v\in L(\mathbb{F}_{2})$ that is freely independent from $w$. Combined with previous results, we conclude that for an arbitrary finitely generated torsion-free non-elementary discrete subgroup $Γ\subset PSL_{2}(\mathbb{R})$, $L(Γ)$ is a free group factor, settling a conjecture of de la Harpe and Voiculescu. This result was obtained using OpenAI's ChatGPT Pro 6.0.

</details>


### [140] [On AF- and type I-ideals in certain crossed product C$^\ast$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11297)
*Alexander G. Ravnanger*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 研究了完全非连通空间交叉积与一致Roe代数中的局部有限维理想，给出动力学与粗几何刻画，并讨论type I理想与UHF代数嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部有限维理想与type I理想在算子代数结构理论中具有核心地位，但交叉积与一致Roe代数背景下缺乏统一的动力学与粗几何刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 分别针对完全非连通空间上整数自由作用的交叉积，以及精确离散群的一致Roe代数，利用动力学描述与粗几何刻画研究局部有限维理想，并考察最大type I理想及紧理想的性质。

Result: 在交叉积情形，最大局部有限维理想可由动力学完全描述，且恰等于所有极大理想的交；在一致Roe代数情形，给出局部有限维紧理想的粗几何特征，并证明精确群自由作用交叉积的最大type I理想是局部有限维的；进一步给出紧理想为type I以及可嵌入UHF代数的粗几何条件。

Conclusion: 建立了局部有限维理想在交叉积与一致Roe代数中的动力学及粗几何刻画，揭示了最大type I理想与局部有限维性的联系，并为紧理想的type I性与UHF嵌入提供判据。

Abstract: We study locally finite-dimensional ideals in crossed products of totally disconnected spaces by free actions of the integers and in uniform Roe algebras of exact discrete groups. In the first case, we present a dynamical description of the largest locally finite-dimensional ideal, which turns out to coincide with the intersection of all maximal ideals. In the latter case, we provide a coarse geometric characterization of the locally finite-dimensional compact ideals. Moreover, we show that for crossed products of totally disconnected spaces by free actions of exact groups, the largest type I-ideal is locally finite-dimensional. In the case of uniform Roe algebras, we provide coarse geometric conditions for compact ideals guaranteeing that the ideal is type I and admits an embedding of a UHF-algebra, respectively.

</details>


### [141] [Continuous family of compact quantum metric space structures from cocycle twisted crossed product $\textrm{C}^{\ast}$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11316)
*Arnab Chattopadhyay,Soumalya Joardar*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 在离散群作用的余圈扭量交叉积上构造三参数族紧量子度量空间结构，并研究其连续性、度量维数界的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究作用于具有指数/次指数增长性质的群时，紧量子度量空间结构的参数族是否具有良好的连续性与维数行为，并探讨度量维数相对于量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离的下半连续性失效问题。

Method: 有余圈扭量的离散群交叉积上建立三参数紧量子度量空间族；在作用群为exact的情形下证明该族关于参数联合连续；给出相关度量维数的定量上、下界。

Result: 证明了参数族的联合连续性（作用群exact时），得到度量维数的定量上下界；这些界有助于证明度量维数关于量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离的下半连续性失效；同时证明度量维数在零量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离下不变。

Conclusion: 该工作为余圈扭量交叉积提供了可控的量子度量结构参数族，并揭示了量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离下度量维数的（非）连续性与不变性特征。

Abstract: We establish the existence of a three-parameter family of compact quantum metric space structures on cocycle twisted crossed products by discrete groups. We are mainly interested in the case where the acting group has exponential/subexponential growth. We prove that the family is jointly continuous with respect to the parameters when the acting group is exact. We obtain quantitative upper and lower bounds for the associated metric dimensions. In particular, the bounds are helpful to prove the failure of lower semicontinuity of the metric dimension with respect to the quantum Gromov-Hausdorff distance. We also prove invariance of metric dimension under zero quantum Gromov-Hausdorff distance.

</details>


### [142] [The classification of flows on $\mathrm{II}_1$ factors and Connes' bicentralizer problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11462)
*Cyril Houdayer,Amine Marrakchi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文解决了冯诺依曼代数中两个长期未决问题：超有限II₁因子上具有完全Connes谱的外流具有Rokhlin性质（从而由Masuda-Tomatsu工作得到分类唯一性），并最终解决了所有III₁因子的Connes双中心化子猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超有限II₁因子上流的分类问题（Takesaki分类问题）以及III₁因子的Connes双中心化子猜想是冯诺依曼代数领域长期悬而未决的核心问题，需要新的工具和理论框架来突破。

Method: 借鉴III型理论，为局部紧群的保迹作用发展双中心化子机制；在顺从情形下将双中心化子猜想与Rokhlin性质相联系；对交换群证明Connes-Størmer传递性定理的类比并推广Connes-Takesaki相对交换子定理；揭示一种新的共振现象。

Result: 证明超有限II₁因子上具完全Connes谱的外流具有Rokhlin性质，结合Masuda-Tomatsu工作得到该流的cocycle共轭唯一性；对ℝ作用的双中心化子猜想给出解决；对所有III₁因子解决Connes双中心化子猜想。

Conclusion: 通过建立保迹作用的双中心化子机制并发现新的共振现象，统一解决了超有限II₁因子上流的分类问题及III₁因子的Connes双中心化子猜想两大公开问题。

Abstract: We settle two long-standing open problems in von Neumann algebras. First, we show that every outer flow with full Connes spectrum on the hyperfinite $\mathrm{II}_1$ factor has the Rokhlin property. By the work of Masuda and Tomatsu, such a flow is therefore unique up to cocycle conjugacy. This settles Takesaki's classification problem for flows on the hyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_1$ factor. Drawing on type $\mathrm{III}$ theory, we develop a bicentralizer machinery for trace-preserving actions of locally compact groups. In the amenable case, we relate the bicentralizer conjecture to the Rokhlin property. For abelian groups, we prove an analog of Connes-Størmer transitivity theorem and we generalize Connes-Takesaki relative commutant theorem. A new resonance phenomenon is revealed which allows us to solve the bicentralizer conjecture for actions of $\R$. We then go back to the type $\mathrm{III}$ world and use this new resonance phenomenon to solve Connes' bicentralizer conjecture for all type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factors.

</details>
